## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route for an investor audience: transaction overview, financial rationale, value creation, standalone EverBank, then an appendix, with section dividers and a consistent blue-and-green identity and header bars. Slides 6, 11 and 25 show disciplined execution, with large key numbers, readable bars and donuts, and clear takeaways. The weaknesses are consequential: many slides (7, 9, 12, 16, 18, 22, 24, 26, 36, 41, 42) pack in tiny text and charts, the footnotes are nearly unreadable, and several titles are topic labels or generic claims, which makes the page-to-page argument harder to follow. Slide 11 repeats the loans and deposits donuts with a decorative icon column and uneven white space, and slide 27 mixes busy logos and checklists.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent header-bar system, the large-number KPI tiles on slides 6 and 25, the blue-green palette, and the clear section dividers and sequence from overview to rationale to appendix.

**Most useful repair:** Cut the density on the pages with tiny text (slides 7, 12, 16, 18, 22, 24, 36, 41, 42) by splitting them or enlarging the key figures, and raise the footnotes to a legible size.

**What would change the score:** Rewrite the topic-label titles (for example slides 19, 21, 26, 30 and 33 to 35) as takeaways, and trim the legal pages on slides 2 and 3 to a short summary with the full text moved to the end.

_Confidence high. 43 of 43 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is an investor presentation for WaFd's proposed merger with EverBank, dated September 7, 2026. It opens with a title slide and then a long forward-looking statements disclaimer and a note on presenters, followed by a transaction overview that sets out the combined bank's scale, multi-channel model and key deal terms such as the 100% stock structure, ownership split and anticipated 1Q'27 close. The middle sections cover the financial rationale, including EPS accretion, cost synergies, integration timeline, capital and liquidity, credit quality, and value creation scenarios based on price-to-tangible-book comparisons. Standalone profiles of EverBank's regional, national and digital channels, its deposit franchise and its management team follow in the main body. The appendix holds detailed channel descriptions, loan and deposit mix, non-depository and commercial real estate exposure, tangible book value and goodwill reconciliations, and the EPS accretion calculation. The deck ends with a closing slide reading Stronger Together. Several slides, including the channel mix and purchase accounting pages, show how complex financial data is laid out for an investor audience.

## Questions about this deck

**What are the key transaction details in the WaFd, Inc. and EverBank Financial Corp investor deck?**

Slide 9 says the deal is 100% common stock consideration, with an ownership split of 59.2% EverBank and 40.8% WaFd. Slide 9 also gives an anticipated closing in 1Q'27.

**What EPS accretion and return figures does the WaFd, Inc. and EverBank Financial Corp deck show?**

Slide 7 shows ~29% 2027E EPS accretion and 15%+ 2027E ROATCE, both based on fully-synergized profitability metrics. Slide 7 also lists a 2.0 years TBV earnback period.

**How large is the combined franchise in the WaFd, Inc. and EverBank Financial Corp presentation?**

Slide 6 describes the combined franchise as a $75bn-asset, multi-channel bank with $59bn in deposits, 82% of which are insured, and $58bn in loans. Slide 6 also notes that financial data is as of June 30, 2026.

**How many slides does the WaFd, Inc. and EverBank Financial Corp deck have and how is it organized?**

The deck has 43 pages. Slide 2 is the forward-looking information statement, slide 4 introduces today's presenters, and slide 14 covers senior management with the board of directors.

Statement Regarding Forward-looking Information
This communication contains certain “forward-looking statements” within the meaning of the Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995, Section 27A of the Securities Act of 1933, as amended (the “Securities Act”), and Section 21E of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, as amended (the “Exchange Act”) with respect to the beliefs, plans, goals, expectations and estimates of WaFd, Inc. (“WaFd”) and EverBank Financial Corp (“EverBank”). Forward-looking statements are not a representation of historical information, but instead pertain to future operations, strategies, financial results or other developments. The words “believe,” “expect,” “anticipate,” “intend,” “target,” “plan,” “estimate,” “should,” “likely,” “will,” “going forward” and other expressions that indicate future events and trends identify forward-looking statements.
Forward-looking statements are necessarily based upon estimates and assumptions that are inherently subject to significant business, operational, economic and competitive uncertainties and contingencies, many of which are beyond the control of WaFd and EverBank, and many of which, with respect to future business decisions and actions, are subject to change and which could cause actual results to differ materially from those contemplated or implied by forward-looking statements or historical performance. Examples of uncertainties and contingencies include factors previously disclosed in WaFd’s reports filed with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (the “SEC”), as well as the following factors, among others: (i) the occurrence of any event, change or other circumstances that could give rise to the right of one or both of the parties to terminate the definitive merger agreement between WaFd and EverBank; (ii) the outcome of any legal proceedings that may be instituted against WaFd or EverBank, including potential litigation that may be instituted against WaFd or its directors or officers related to the proposed transaction or the definitive merger agreement between WaFd and EverBank; (iii) the timing and completion of the transaction, including the possibility that the proposed transaction will not close when expected or at all because required regulatory, shareholder or other approvals are not received or other conditions to the closing are not satisfied on a timely basis or at all, or are obtained subject to conditions that are not anticipated; (iv) the risk that any announcements relating to the proposed combination could have adverse effects on the market price of the common stock of WaFd; (v) the possibility that the anticipated benefits of the transaction will not be realized when expected or at all, including as a result of the impact of, or problems arising from, the integration of the two companies or as a result of the strength of the economy and competitive factors in the areas where WaFd and EverBank do business; (vi) certain restrictions during the pendency of the merger that may impact the parties’ ability to pursue certain business opportunities or strategic transactions; (vii) the possibility that the transaction may be more expensive to complete than anticipated, including as a result of unexpected factors or events; (viii) diversion of management’s attention from ongoing business operations and opportunities; (ix) reputational risk and potential adverse reactions or changes to business or employee relationships, including those resulting from the announcement or completion of the transaction; (x) WaFd’s and EverBank’s success in executing their respective business plans and strategies and managing the risks involved in the foregoing; (xi) currency and interest rate fluctuations; (xii) success of hedging activities; (xiii) material adverse changes in economic and industry conditions, including the availability of short and long-term financing; (xiv) general competitive, economic, political and market conditions; (xv) changes in asset quality and credit risk; (xvi) the inability to sustain revenue and earnings growth; (xvii) inflation; (xviii) customer borrowing, repayment, investment and deposit practices; (xix) the impact, extent and timing of technological changes; (xx) capital management activities; (xxi) other actions of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, the Office of the Comptroller of the Currency and the State of Washington; (xxii) legislative and regulatory actions and reforms; and (xxiii) other factors that may affect future results of WaFd and EverBank.
We caution that the foregoing list of important factors that may affect future results is not exhaustive. Additional factors that could cause results to differ materially from those contemplated by forward-looking statements can be found in WaFd’s Annual Report on Form 10-K for the fiscal year ended September 30, 2025, and in its subsequent Quarterly Reports on Form 10-Q filed with the SEC and available in the “Investor Relations” section of WaFd’s website, www.wafdbank.com/about-us/investor-relations, under the heading “SEC Filings” and in other documents WaFd files with the SEC (available at www.sec.gov). All such factors, as well as other uncertainties and potential events, and the inherent uncertainty of forward-looking statements, should be considered carefully when making decisions with respect to WaFd and EverBank.
Any forward-looking statements contained in this document represent the views of WaFd and EverBank only as of the date hereof and are presented for the purpose of assisting their respective shareholders and analysts in understanding WaFd’s and EverBank’s financial position, objectives and priorities and anticipated financial performance as at and for the periods ended on the dates presented, and may not be appropriate for other purposes. Neither WaFd nor EverBank undertakes to update any forward-looking statements, whether written or oral, that may be made from time to time by or on its behalf, except as required under applicable securities legislation.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Rewrite the topic-label titles (for example slides 19, 21, 26, 30 and 33 to 35) as takeaways, and trim the legal pages on slides 2 and 3 to a short summary with the full text moved to the end.

**Text**

Important Other Information
In connection with the proposed transaction, WaFd intends to file relevant materials with the SEC, including a proxy statement on Schedule 14A. Promptly after filing its definitive proxy statement with the SEC, WaFd will mail the definitive proxy statement to each shareholder entitled to vote at the meeting relating to the proposed transaction.
This communication does not constitute an offer to sell or a solicitation of an offer to buy any securities or a solicitation of any vote or approval. SHAREHOLDERS OF WAFD ARE URGED TO READ, WHEN AVAILABLE, ALL RELEVANT DOCUMENTS (INCLUDING ANY AMENDMENTS OR SUPPLEMENTS THERETO) FILED WITH THE SEC, INCLUDING WAFD’S PROXY STATEMENT, BECAUSE THEY WILL CONTAIN IMPORTANT INFORMATION ABOUT WAFD AND THE PROPOSED TRANSACTION.
Investors and shareholders of WaFd will be able to obtain a free copy of the proxy statement as well as other relevant documents filed with the SEC without charge at the SEC’s website (http://www.sec.gov). Copies of the proxy statement and the filings with the SEC that will be incorporated by reference in the proxy statement can also be obtained, without charge, by directing a request to Brad Goode, WaFd, Inc., 425 Pike Street, Seattle, Washington 98101, telephone (206) 626-8178.
Participants in the Solicitation
WaFd, EverBank and certain of WaFd’s directors and executive officers may be deemed to be participants in the solicitation of proxies in respect of the proposed transaction under the rules of the SEC. Information regarding WaFd’s directors and executive officers is available in the proxy statement for its 2026 annual meeting of shareholders, which was filed with the SEC, and certain of its Current Reports on Form 8-K. Other information regarding the participants in the proxy solicitation and a description of their direct and indirect interests, by security holdings or otherwise, will be contained in the proxy statement and other relevant materials to be filed with the SEC when they become available. Free copies of these documents, when available, may be obtained as described in the preceding paragraph.
Non-GAAP Financial Measures
This communication contains certain non-GAAP financial measures that are not in accordance with U.S. generally accepted accounting principles (GAAP). WaFd uses certain non-GAAP financial measures to provide meaningful, supplemental information regarding its operational results and to enhance investors’ overall understanding of WaFd’s financial performance. The limitations associated with non-GAAP financial measures include the risk that persons might disagree as to the appropriateness of items comprising these measures and that different companies might calculate these measures differently. These disclosures should not be considered an alternative to WaFd’s GAAP results.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Pairs side-by-side company descriptions with headline metric callouts in a clean two-column layout that makes the combined scale easy to read at a glance.
- Review: Slides 6, 11 and 25 show disciplined execution, with large key numbers, readable bars and donuts, and clear takeaways.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent header-bar system, the large-number KPI tiles on slides 6 and 25, the blue-green palette, and the clear section dividers and sequence from overview to rationale to appendix.

**Text**

Bringing EverBank and WaFd Together
National, Scaled, Multi-channel Banking Franchise with Robust Profitability
Key Highlights
EverBank is a $47bn-asset, scaled digital bank with branches in Florida & California and deep national commercial lending expertise
WaFd is a $28bn-asset, relationship-focused regional bank located in attractive Western U.S. markets
Combined franchise is a $75bn-asset, multi-channel bank with scale, diverse products and a branch footprint in highly attractive markets
Combination enhances both franchises, providing lending and funding diversification, accelerating profitability ramp and unlocking growth upside
EverBank’s proven track record in commercial lending complements WaFd’s business banking strategy and accelerates the 2030 plan
WaFd’s relationship-based banking provides attractive funding diversification to EverBank’s highly sticky digital deposits
Together, the franchise is well positioned with the profitability, scale, products, channels and markets to be a high performing bank

**Layout**

Title line at top left with inline logos, subtitle beneath. Left column (about 43% wide) holds a blue header bar and seven stacked light-grey highlight rows. Right column holds a header bar, a row of three blue tiles, a second header bar and a row of three light-blue tiles. A full-width navy banner sits below both columns, then a thin rule with footer logos, source and note text, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

EverBank logo (blue stacked-chevron mark with 'EverBank' in dark grey) inline in the title between 'Bringing' and 'and'. WaFd logo (green outlined starburst mark with green bold 'WaFd') inline between 'and' and 'Together'. Top stat tiles with soft drop shadows in graduated periwinkle blues, light to darker left to right. Bottom stat tiles with soft drop shadows in sky blue, light cyan on the right. Footer has a small blue EverBank chevron icon, a thin vertical divider and a small green WaFd icon. A thin grey horizontal rule runs above the footer. Navy banner has a subtle shadow and rounded corners.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Calibri, bright blue #1751E8, about 4.8% of slide height. Subtitle in Arial regular, near-black #181818, about 3% of slide height. Header bars in royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial, about 2.2%, slightly rounded corners. Highlight rows in #F5F7F8 with Arial 2.2% body text in #181818 and key phrases in bold navy #142571. Big stat numbers in Arial bold navy #142571 at 5.6%, labels bold navy at 2%, and '82% insured' in bold italic at 1.5%. The bottom banner is navy #142571 with white bold Arial at 2.6%. Footer text in Arial 1.1% blue #1751E8. Page number in Calibri 1.5% cyan #00A3E0. Tiles are square-cornered with soft shadows.

A narrow 'Attractive Pricing' column with two bar charts sits at the right. Two small bar charts in the right column. Chart 1, '2027 Price / Earnings': three bars on a black baseline, green (10.8x, tallest), navy (8.9x) and bright blue (7.2x), with value labels above each. Chart 2, 'Price / TBVPS': two bars on a black baseline, green (1.2x) and navy (1.1x), with the same logos under each and the caption '~90% pay-to-trade ratio'. The label cells have a thick navy vertical bar on their right edge. The bottom banner has a soft shadow.

**Text**

EverBank + WaFd : Stronger Together
Bringing Together Complementary Institutions to Create a Highly Profitable and High-Growth Franchise
1
Multi-channel, relationship-driven strategy pairing a regional bank with a national, digital and commercial lending franchise
2

3

EverBank
+ WaFd
4
Strong EPS accretion, return profile, and earnings power support robust growth and capital return
5
Efficient cost structure and flexible funding model enable outperformance across economic cycles
6
Experienced management team with significant integration experience and deep local and national expertise
7
Scarcity value underpinned by diverse business model, robust profitability, and scale in attractive markets
8

**Layout**

Title line with logos at top left, subtitle beneath. Seven horizontal pale-blue bands stacked down the right, each with a numbered circle on the left that follows a set of concentric arcs bulging from the left edge. A thin grey rule and small logo sit in the footer, and the page number is at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Concentric circular arcs on the left, cut off by the left edge: outermost royal blue #1751E8, then navy #14246E, then cyan #00AEEF, then green #00A040 around a light grey #F2F2F2 half-disc. Inside the disc: EverBank logo (blue stacked-bars mark with dark grey wordmark) and below it a grey plus sign and the green WaFd logo with a seal mark. Seven white circles with thick blue outlines, numbered 1-7, are placed along the arcs, forming a left-bulging curve with 1 and 7 farthest left and 4 farthest right. Seven horizontal bands with a pale blue-lavender gradient that fades to white on the right. The title carries the EverBank logo, a blue plus, and the green WaFd logo. The footer has a small blue EverBank mark, a thin vertical divider and a small green WaFd seal icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #1751E8 Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Subtitle in regular Arial #181818, about 3% of height. Band text in bold Arial #181818, about 2.2% of height. Numbers in bold Arial blue #1751E8, about 3.6%. The bands are flat pale gradient rectangles with no outline. The circles have a white fill and a blue stroke of about 4px. The footer rule is a thin grey line. The page number is cyan #00A3E0.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Key Transaction Details
Structure
WaFd, Inc. issues shares to EverBank Financial Corp stockholders
WaFd, Inc. will remain a publicly traded bank holding company, renamed EverBank Financial Corp under a new ticker – EVBK
WaFd will be the legal acquiror and EverBank will be the accounting acquiror (WaFd’s balance sheet will be subject to fair value accounting)
EverBank Financial Corp will be regulated by the Federal Reserve and EverBank, N.A. by the OCC
Consideration
100% common stock consideration
WaFd will issue 103.1mm shares(1) (107.7mm inclusive of options) in connection with the transaction
Pro forma shares: 177.1mm basic | 182.0mm diluted
Ownership split: 59.2% EverBank / 40.8% WaFd
Brand
Holding company
Regional
National
Digital
EverBank
West region: WaFd
CA / FL: EverBank
EverBank
EB Direct (pending)
Headquarters
Holding company: Bellevue, WA | Bank: Jacksonville, FL
Board of Directors
7 EverBank / 6 WaFd Directors
Chairman: Robert Radway | Directors include Greg Seibly and Brent Beardall
Leadership:
Chief Executive Officer: Greg Seibly
President: Brent Beardall
Conditions / approvals
Subject to receipt of WaFd shareholder approval
Approval of EverBank stockholders has been obtained
Subject to customary regulatory approvals
EverBank stockholders entitled to customary registration rights and have agreed to a phased lock up schedule over 12 months post closing(2)
Anticipated closing in 1Q’27
Source: Company filings; FactSet; S&P Global Market Intelligence
Notes:
1. Number of WaFd shares to be issued at closing will depend on the number of fully diluted shares of WaFd as of closing (calculated using the treasury stock method based on the 10-day VWAP of the WaFd shares prior to closing) to target an ownership split of 59.2% to EverBank and 40.8% to WaFd; includes additional dilutive commitments; 2. Four-period lock up beginning at closing, with shares released 10% / 10% / 40% / 40% after 30 days, 90 days, 180 days and 12 months, respectively
9

**Layout**

Title at top left. Below it a seven-row table: a pale blue label column on the left (about 5% to 20% of width) and a white content column to the right with bullet lists, rows separated by thin grey horizontal rules. The Brand row holds four columns with blue header bars. Footer has a thin grey rule, small logos at bottom left, source and notes text indented at about 15% of width, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Seven pale blue label cells (#E4EAFC) with a thick dark navy vertical bar at each cell's right edge. Four dark blue rounded header bars in the Brand row (Holding company, Regional, National, Digital) with white bold text. Blue circular bullets before each item. Thin grey horizontal divider lines between rows and above the footer. Two small logos at the bottom left: a blue stylised 'E' EverBank mark, then a thin vertical divider, then a green circular wreath-style WaFd mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #1751E8 Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Row labels bold Arial in navy #142571. Body text Arial regular in navy #142571, about 1.84% of slide height, with bold lead-ins. Bullets blue #1751E8. Header bars #113DAE with white bold Arial. Footnotes small Arial in blue #1751E8 at about 1.1%. Page number in cyan-blue #00A3E0 Calibri. Flat shapes, header bars with small corner radius, no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Title top-left across the slide. Below it, a wide blue header bar over the left map panel (about 59% width) and a narrower header bar over the right 2x3 grid of stat tiles. Map of the western US on the left, with two circular inset maps (Texas, Florida) stacked in a white panel in the middle. Footer has a thin grey rule, logos at bottom-left, source and notes starting at about 15% x, and page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: a map of the western US (WA, OR, CA, NV, ID, MT, WY, UT, CO, AZ, NM) filled pale blue #D1DCFA, with MT, WY and CO in grey #DDDCDB. Green dots mark the 212 branches of one bank and blue dots mark the 42 EverBank branches. Green dots are concentrated around Seattle, Portland, Salem, Boise and Phoenix and scattered across the interior states. Blue dots cluster around San Francisco/San Jose, Los Angeles/Long Beach and in Nevada. A legend at top-left shows a green dot with a green star-like logo (212) and a blue dot with a blue stacked E logo (42). Middle: a white panel with a subtle shadow holding two circular insets with blue (#1751E8) outlines. The Texas inset shows a pale blue Texas map with a few green dots near Ft. Worth/Dallas and Austin. The Florida inset shows a pale blue Florida map with blue dots at Jacksonville, Tampa, Naples, West Palm Beach and Miami. Right: six pale blue tiles (#D1DCFA) with soft drop shadows in a 2x3 grid. Footer: blue stacked E logo, a thin vertical divider and a green star-like logo at bottom-left, and a thin grey horizontal rule above the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, bold Calibri, large. Header bars are royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text and slightly rounded corners. Stat tiles use navy #142571 bold Arial, with large numbers and smaller descriptors, on pale blue #D1DCFA with soft shadow. Map labels are small navy Arial, with bold state abbreviations. Footnotes are tiny Arial in blue #1751E8. Page number is cyan #00A3E0 in Calibri.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses three paired composition charts for loans and deposits across regional, national and digital channels, so the channel relationship is visible in one view.
- Review: Slides 6, 11 and 25 show disciplined execution, with large key numbers, readable bars and donuts, and clear takeaways.
- Review: Slide 11 repeats the loans and deposits donuts with a decorative icon column and uneven white space, and slide 27 mixes busy logos and checklists.

**Text**

Complementary Multi-Channel Strategy Leveraging the Strength of Both Franchises
Channel Overview
Composition
Regional
Core, in-market relationship banking / franchise built on WaFd’s 110-year history
National
Relationship driven commercial lending / focused on national industry verticals with / attractive risk-adjusted returns
Digital
Nationwide digital bank complementing / strategic branch footprint providing / durable funding and balance sheet / resilience

**Layout**

Title across the top. Below, two panels: a left 'Channel Overview' column with three stacked rows (Regional, National, Digital), each with a circular icon on the left and a coloured label bar with italic description. A right 'Composition' panel (white card with shadow, bleeding off the right edge) holds two sections, Loans and Deposits, each with checkmark bullets on the left and a donut chart on the right. Thin grey rule above a footer with logos at left, source/note text at centre-left and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left column: three light grey circular backgrounds (#F5F7F8) each with a large pale ring outline. Ring 1 is light blue (#CCEEFF) with an arrow tip pointing down-left toward the next ring; ring 2 is lavender; ring 3 is pale periwinkle with a small dot at its top. Icons: a light-blue line-art handshake (Regional), a teal line-art outline of the United States map (National), and a teal line-art tablet/phone device (Digital). Right panel: two donut charts with soft shadows and white centres. Loans donut ($58B): Regional 32% in bright blue (#00AAFF) on the upper right, National 68% in dark navy (#142571) for the rest. Deposits donut ($59B): Regional 53% bright blue (#00AAFF) on the right, Digital 30% royal blue (#1751E8) on the upper left, National 16% dark navy (#142571) at the bottom left. Dark blue header bars for Loans and Deposits are partially overlapped by the donuts. Dark navy checkmarks beside each bullet. Footer logos: a blue stylised E mark, a thin vertical divider, and a green outlined emblem.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue (#1751E8) bold Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Section header bars are royal navy blue (#113DAE) with white bold Arial text, rounded corners, about 2.6% size. Body text Arial 2.2% in grey (#3B3B3B) with key phrases bold navy (#142571); descriptions are italic navy. Label bars are pale tints: light blue #CCEEFF, lavender #BFC9F4, periwinkle #D1DCFA. Chart labels bold Arial with bold-italic percentages, coloured to match their slices. Footer text small blue Arial. Card with a soft drop shadow and rounded top-left corner.

**Layout**

Two-column layout under a two-line left-aligned title. Left column Lending and right column Deposits, each with a dark blue header bar, a pale lavender subheader band and a light grey rounded panel holding a donut chart and two dashed-outline callout pills. Three equal light-blue takeaway boxes span the width below. A thin grey rule, footer logos, source notes and page number at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Left: donut chart for the $58B lending portfolio with segments clockwise from the top: CRE 12% (bright blue #1751E8), Multi-family 16% (dark navy #142571), Corporate debt finance 7% (mid blue #0A82BE), Fund finance 11% (light sky blue #66CCFF), Lender finance 2% (very pale blue #99DDFF), Structured mortgage finance 6% (very dark teal #1F3B3B), C&I 16% (teal #68ADAD), C&D 4% (green #3CC878), Residential 25% (grey #B0B0B0), plus a tiny Consumer sliver at the top. A blue dashed arc with blue dot end markers runs around the commercial segments, from the top near Consumer round clockwise to C&D. A black dotted arc with black dot markers runs round the Residential/Legacy runoff segment on the upper left. Right: donut chart for the $59B deposits: Financial centers 43% (bright blue #1751E8), Consumer direct 30% (lavender #BFC9F4), Commercial direct and specialty 16% (pale blue #CCEEFF), Other 11% (grey-teal #9AC2C2). Centre text of each donut is bold italic blue. Four callout pills with white fill and blue dashed outlines. Three light-blue takeaway boxes with drop shadows. Footer logos: a blue stylised E mark, a thin vertical divider and a green outlined emblem. Thin grey horizontal rule above the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Calibri blue #1751E8, about 4.8% of slide height. Section header bars dark blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text. Subheader bands #D8DEF8 with navy bold Arial text (#142571 and #2B3B81). Panels very light grey (#F2F2F2 left, #F7F7F7 right), left with large rounded corners. Chart labels bold Arial dark grey #404040 and white percentages inside segments. Callout pills have blue #1751E8 bold Arial text. Takeaway boxes #CCEEFF with navy #142571 bold italic Arial text, soft grey shadow. Footnotes small Arial blue. Page number Calibri cyan-blue #00A3E0.

13

**Layout**

Title top-left. Left panel (light grey) holds a two-circle Venn diagram. To its right are three tall rounded cards in a row (green, dark grey, light blue), each with a check badge at the top-right corner. Two wide light-grey rounded boxes sit below the cards. Footer has a thin grey rule, logos at bottom-left, source and note text, and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Venn diagram: two overlapping white circles with thick light-grey outlines, one above the other. The lens-shaped overlap is filled grey and labelled ~15% ROATCE. Three white circles with pale green outlines each contain a green check mark, overlapping the top-right corners of the cards. Footer logos: a blue stylised E mark, a thin vertical divider, and a green outlined emblem.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is bright blue #1751E8 in bold Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Card text is Arial. The green card (#DBF5E3) uses dark green #1F6C38 text, the dark card (#474C50) uses white text, and the light-blue card (#CCEEFF) uses navy #142571 text. Big figures are bold Arial at about 5.2% of slide height. Card descriptions and captions are regular Arial at about 3.3%. Venn labels are bold caps Arial, green #297341 at top and navy #142571 at bottom. The centre label is #2B4F4F. The cards and bottom boxes have rounded corners and no shadows. The footer text is tiny blue Arial, and the page number is cyan #00A3E0 Calibri.

**Layout**

Title across top left. Left column (about 5–52% wide) has two light-blue career-history bands for Greg Seibly and Brent Beardall, then two blue header bars each followed by logo rows: past employers and award badges. Right column (53–94%) has a blue 'Leadership Structure' header, a board-of-directors panel and a grey management-team panel with six profile cards. Footer rule with logos bottom left, source line and page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Circular headshot photos of each executive (Greg Seibly, Brent Beardall, Robert Radway, Pat Rusnak, Seth Waller, Mercy Anne Martin, Mark Baum, Kim Robison) with blue or green ring borders. EverBank blue stacked-E logo and WaFd green logo are used as tags on cards. Past-employer logos: Union Bank, FHLBank San Francisco, Umpqua, Sterling Bank, Bank of America, U.S. Bancorp, Wells Fargo, Deloitte, WaFd, Sterling, TIAA Bank, TIAA, Luther Burbank. Award badges: BauerFinancial five-star gold column emblem, Kiplinger Best Banks 2024 badge, Forbes Best-in-State Banks 2025 black plaque, green Best Banks 2025 ribbon. Board bar: blue segment labelled 7 EverBank and green segment labelled WaFd 6. White cards with a light shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a bright blue title (#1751E8) in Calibri bold, about 4.4% of slide height. Header bars are royal blue (#113DAE) with white bold Arial text. Navy (#142571) names and text, grey-blue (#5D6986) titles and years. EverBank blue #1751E8 and WaFd green #009639 accents. Panels are pale lavender (#EBEEFC, #BEC8F4 at 30% opacity) and light grey (#F2F2F2). Cards are white with a light grey border and slight shadow. The left bands have a rounded bottom-right corner.

### Slide 15

**Text**

Overview of Standalone EverBank
15

**Layout**

Section divider slide with a full-bleed abstract wave image. The title is left-aligned in the left half at about 46% from the top, with a left margin of about 5.7%. A small page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract illustration of flowing translucent ribbon waves. Blue ribbons (from #0A6AD8 to pale #DCE8F7) sweep from the top-left corner and curve down toward the centre. Green ribbons (about #2E9A3E fading to pale #E3F1E3) sweep from the centre-right toward the bottom-right corner. Thin white and pale lines run along the ribbon edges, and fine striations show on the lower blue and green bands. Large areas of near-white (#F8F9FA) remain in the upper right and lower left. A faint grey-white curved band crosses the right edge.

**Design**

Background: Near-white #F8F9FA with a full-bleed blue and green abstract wave image over a #FFFFFF fill. Near-white background with a blue-to-green wave graphic. The title is bold royal blue #1751E8 in Calibri Bold, about 5.2% of slide height. The page number is Calibri in cyan-blue #00A3E0, about 1.5% of slide height. There are no shadows or borders, and the ribbons are soft with semi-transparent gradients.

16

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left on white. Three columns below, each headed by a dark blue banner: left KPI grid and bar charts on pale blue panel, middle deposit stats on grey panel with donut chart and a grey callout box, right stacked bars, a ring diagram and a grey callout box. Footer rule with logos at bottom left, source and notes text, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: two bar charts in navy. Loans ($B) has two bars, 2023 $26 and 2Q26 $37, with a bracket arrow labelled CAGR: 12%. ROAA has four bars of increasing height: 2023 0.28%, 2024 0.59%, 2025 0.71%, YTD 2026 0.96%. Two periwinkle label buttons, Growth and Profitability, sit above them. Middle: donut chart with centre label $38B, segments Financial Centers 27% (bright blue), Consumer Digital 47% (light periwinkle), Commercial & Specialty 13% (light cyan), Sweeps & Other 13% (light grey). Right: two stacked bars. Loan Mix 2Q26: Commercial 57% navy, Commercial Real Estate 23% cyan, Residential 20% blue. Origination Mix 2Q26: 68% navy, 32% bright cyan. Below them is a ring diagram of blue wedges with white labels (Structured Real Estate, Lender Finance, CRE Bridge, Fund Finance, Multi-family, Corporate Debt Finance, C&I, Structured Mortgage Finance, Equipment Lending) and pale wedges for Residential Mortgage and Legacy Comm. Real Estate. A green dotted arc with green end dots surrounds the core lending wedges, and a pale dotted arc marks Legacy Runoff. The EverBank logo is in the centre of the ring. Footer has the small blue EverBank logo mark and a green outline emblem separated by a thin vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Calibri-style sans, bright blue #1751E8. Subtitle in Arial, near-black #181818. Banners in #113DAE with white bold Arial text. KPI numbers in Franklin Gothic Book, blue #1751E8, with dark labels #181818. Navy #142571 bars, cyan #00AAFF accents, green #4CCB75 for core lending. Panels are pale blue #ECF1FC and grey #F2F2F2. Callout boxes have rounded corners. Footnotes are tiny blue Arial. A thin grey rule sits above the footer. No shadows.

**Layout**

Title top-left across the slide. Below, two columns: left (about 5–54% wide) has two header bars, a 3x2 grid of stat tiles, then four checkmark bullets; right (55–95%) has a header bar, a chart subtitle strip and a bar chart panel. A full-width italic takeaway banner sits below, then a thin rule and a footer with logos, source notes and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, 'QoQ Deposit Growth During the 2023 Liquidity Panic', clustered columns of 1Q23 (light blue #7497F1) and 2Q23 (dark navy #142571) around a zero baseline. Legend top right with square swatches. EverBank (highlighted in a light blue rounded panel with blue outline and shadow): 1Q23 about 1% (above zero), 2Q23 2%. KRX quartiles in a grey rounded panel: Bottom quartile 1Q23 (5%), 2Q23 (3%); Middle quartile 1Q23 (3%), 2Q23 (2%); Top quartile 1Q23 (1%), 2Q23 1%. KRX label with a thin navy rule above it. EverBank logo (blue stacked-chevron icon plus 'EverBank' wordmark) at the bottom of the highlighted panel. Six stat tiles with soft shadows: top row pale lavender, bottom row light cyan shades. Four navy checkmark icons beside bullets. Footer has a small blue EverBank icon, a vertical divider and a green circular emblem.

**Design**

**Layout**

Two-line left-aligned title at top. Below, three columns under blue header bars: a left timeline column (history), a middle column of three rounded cards with checkmark bullets, and a right bordered panel with three stacked chart sections. Footer has a thin rule, logos at bottom left, source and notes text, and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: a grey vertical arrow band pointing down with a tip near the bottom, with grey year boxes (1994, 2012, 2017), a navy 2023 box and a bright blue Today box, each with a white border and shadow. Black dot bullets sit beside the italic timeline items. A lavender-grey translucent bar extends from the 2023 box to the middle column, with a tapering lavender-grey wedge joining the timeline to the cards. Middle: three light grey (#F5F7F8) rounded cards with shadows, each with a thick navy vertical bar at its left edge. Right: two donut charts for 2023 ($26B) and 2Q26 ($37B) with a grey right arrow between them. Legend colours: Residential bright blue, CRE navy, Asset-Backed Lending teal blue, Consumer lavender, Structured Mortgage cyan, Equipment Financing dark green-grey, C&I green. 2023 slices: 34% blue, 26% navy, 16% teal, 11% cyan, 13% dark green. 2Q26 slices: 18% blue, 23% navy, 31% teal, 10% cyan, 4% green, 12% dark green, 2% lavender. Below, a bar chart of NIX/Assets with navy bars: 2023 2.09% and 2Q26 1.49%, with pill labels NIX($M) $760(2) and $693. Then a ROAA bar chart: 2023 0.28% and YTD 2026 0.96%, with pills NI($M) $89 and $398 and an arrow labelled 89% CAGR. Grey horizontal divider lines separate the sections. Small logos at bottom left: a blue E mark and a green seal icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is bright blue #1751E8 in bold Calibri. Header bars are royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text. Timeline headings are dark green #2B4F4F, bold Arial. Timeline bullets are italic dark grey #3B3B3B. Card headings and body are navy #142571. Accents are blue #1751E8. Footnotes are small blue Arial text. Cards have large corner radii and soft shadows. The right panel has a rounded grey border.

**Layout**

Title top-left, left-aligned, spanning nearly the full width. Below it a legend row, then a 3-column by 2-row grid of bar-chart panels, each with a blue header bar. A thin grey rule runs across the bottom, with logos and source notes in the footer and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six vertical bar charts with no gridlines or y-axes, only a faint baseline under each and year labels 2023 to 2028 beneath. Bars for 2023-2025 are dark navy (Actual); bars for 2026-2028 are bright blue (Management Forecast) in every chart except Efficiency Ratio, where 2026-2028 are also bright blue. Legend: navy square 'Actual', blue square 'Management Forecast'. Top row: Efficiency Ratio 85%, 65%, 61%, 53%, 51%, 51% (decreasing bars); Net Interest Margin 2.05%, 2.34%, 2.62%, 2.65%, 2.74%, 2.78%; ROAA 0.28%, 0.59%, 0.71%, 0.94%, 0.98%, 1.00%. Each top-row panel has a small dashed blue-outlined callout box under the header: '26 YTD: 53%, '26 YTD: 2.62%, '26 YTD: 0.96%. ### Slide 20

**Text**

Financial Rationale
20

**Layout**

Section divider: a single title left-aligned at the left margin (about 6% from left), vertically near the middle (about 46-51% down). The right half is empty white space. A small page number sits at the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract background image (1536x864 px) of flowing translucent ribbon waves. Blue ribbons (royal blue #0B6BD6 to pale sky blue #BBD2F0) sweep from the top-left corner and taper down toward the centre-right, with fine parallel hairlines at the left edge. They fade into white around the slide centre (x about 50%, y about 55%). Green ribbons (deep green #2E9A3E to pale mint #DDEEDD) emerge from the centre and sweep to the bottom-right corner, with thin white curved lines. Large pale grey-white arcs curve through the right side. The top-right and bottom-left are near-white open space.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photographic wave graphic, blue top-left to green bottom-right, on near-white #F9FAFB. Near-white background #F9FAFB with a blue-to-green wave image. The title is bold royal blue #1751E8 in Calibri Bold, about 5.2% of slide height. The page number is small Calibri in cyan-blue #00A3E0, about 1.5% of slide height. No shadows or shapes beyond the image.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Rewrite the topic-label titles (for example slides 19, 21, 26, 30 and 33 to 35) as takeaways, and trim the legal pages on slides 2 and 3 to a short summary with the full text moved to the end.

**Text**

Key Financial Assumptions
Standalone earnings:
WaFd: Consensus earnings estimates through 2027(1)
EverBank: Management forecast through 2028, as reflected on slide 19
Cost savings:
$135mm annual run-rate pre-tax cost savings (~11% of the combined NIX); grown 3% annually
40% phase-in during 2027, 100% phase-in by 2H 2028
Revenue synergies:
Not modeled in pro forma financial metrics; significant opportunities identified, including insurance and wealth(2)
One-time costs:
$280mm pre-tax (for illustrative purposes, fully reflected in pro forma capital at closing)
Credit mark:
$313mm (1.55% of WaFd’s projected gross loans at closing, 1.45x WaFd’s loan loss reserve at close)
Fair value marks / on WaFd:
($601mm) pre-tax loan mark accreted 10 years using straight-line method
($145mm) pre-tax securities mark accreted 4.5 years using straight-line method
$29mm pre-tax fixed assets mark amortized 40 years using straight-line method
$15mm pre-tax time deposits mark amortized 1 year using straight-line method
$3mm pre-tax other borrowings mark amortized 2 years using straight-line method
$105mm preferred equity mark
CDI:
Core deposit intangible of $357mm, 2.8% of WaFd’s core deposits, amortized over 10 years using sum-of-years digits
Other:
Company plans to enter into hedges to manage interest rate risk between announce and close
Outstanding EverBank options rolled into economic equivalent for the pro forma company
Pro forma metrics reflect illustrative January 1, 2027 closing date
Source: Company filings; FactSet; S&P Global Market Intelligence
Notes:
1. Based on mean analyst consensus estimates as of September 4, 2026; 2. Excludes TIAA depositors which, by agreement, may not be solicited for wealth management services
21

Title top-left. Thin grey horizontal rules separate the rows. A grey footer rule runs across the slide at about 92%, with logos at bottom-left, source and notes text beside them, and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Eight pale blue (#E4EAFC) label cells, each with a dark navy (#1B2A7A) vertical bar on its right edge. Thin grey horizontal divider lines between rows, extending across the right-hand text area. Blue filled circular bullets before each bullet line. A thin grey horizontal line above the footer. Bottom-left shows two small logos separated by a thin vertical line: a blue stylised 'E' (EverBank) mark and a green circular emblem (WaFd).

No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Title top-left. Three header bars: Cost Synergies Schedule (left), Summary of Cost Synergies (centre), Integration Plan (right column). Left holds a bar chart, centre a donut chart. A full-width-left Core Conversion timeline bar spans below, with a grey Key Milestones panel containing six rounded boxes in a row. Right column is bulleted text with bank logos beneath. Thin vertical divider before the right column. Footer rule with logos at bottom left, source/notes at bottom, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart of three columns: 2027E has a solid cyan bar $54 (40% bubble) under a dashed-outline light grey box totalling $135; 4Q 2027 run-rate has cyan $108 under a dashed grey box totalling $135; Mid-year 2028E run-rate has a solid cyan $139 bar (100% bubble), total $139. Legend: dashed outline square = annualized, solid cyan square = realized, light blue oval with % = phased in. Donut chart with $135M centre: Compensation (cyan, about 55%), Technology & occupancy (dark navy, about 25%), General & admin (royal blue, about 20%). Timeline: six navy rounded boxes Q1'27 to H2'28 linked by dotted grey connectors; grey label tags with dashed vertical connectors from Target close date to Q1'27, and from Core conversion date and Integration completion target to H1'28. Logos: EverBank/Sterling, U.S. Bancorp, TIAA Bank, UnionBank, TIAA, Umpqua, WaFd, SterlingBank, Luther Burbank. Footer logos: EverBank mark and green WaFd-style mark separated by vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, Calibri bold, about 4.8% of slide height. Section headers white bold Arial on royal blue #113DAE bars with rounded corners. Body text Arial in blue #1751E8 and navy #142571. Cyan #00AAFF accent for bars and donut. Milestone boxes navy #142571 with white border and soft shadow; grey #F2F2F2 panel with rounded corners; tags #757575 with white text. Bullets are round blue dots.

23

**Layout**

Title and subtitle left-aligned at top with a wide left margin. Below is a 3-column by 2-row grid of chart panels, each with a dark blue header bar and four vertical bars with value labels above and logos plus year labels below. A thin grey rule, footnotes and corner logos run along the bottom, with the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six bar charts, each with four bars and no axes or gridlines: green (first), bright blue (second), and two navy bars (pro forma and 2028 target). Values are printed above the bars. Under each bar is a logo: a green outlined starburst/flower logo (WaFd) for the first bar, a blue stacked-chevron 'E' logo (EverBank) for the second, and for the last two bars both logos separated by a thin navy vertical bar. Efficiency Ratio: 55%, 51%, 45%, ~45%. Noninterest Expense / Assets: 1.65%, 1.49%, 1.47%, ~1.45%. Net Interest Margin: 2.83%, 2.74%, 2.89%, ~2.95%. ROAA: 0.95%, 0.98%, 1.10%, ~1.15%. ROATCE: 10.5%, 11.9%, 15%+, 15%+. Net Income to Common ($mm): $250, $438, $788, $865+. It has an EPS legend with a blue oval marker, and dark blue oval callouts with white text: $3.37 above the first bar and $4.34 above the third bar. Footer left has the EverBank and WaFd logos separated by a vertical line. A thin grey horizontal rule sits above the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, Calibri bold, about 4.8% of slide height. Subtitle in near-black #181818 Arial, 2.6%. Panel headers are rounded-corner rectangles filled #113DAE with white bold Arial text at 2.2%. Bars are flat, with no shadows, in green #37AC61, bright blue #144FEA and navy #142571. Value labels are bold Arial 1.67% in black. Axis labels are Arial 1.58% in black, with 'pro forma' and 'target' in italics. Footnotes are Arial 1.1% in blue #1751E8. The page number is Calibri in cyan #00A3E0.

Back-book redeployment — Redeploy low-yielding legacy assets into / higher risk-adjusted return loans
Balance-sheet scale — A larger combined balance sheet expands / underwriting capacity

Funding optimization — Flexibility to optimize funding mix across / branch and digital channels
Expand insurance income — Cross-selling personal and commercial / insurance across the combined franchise
Wealth cross-sell — WaFd's wealth management offering can be / extended to EverBank’s affluent client base (3)

24

**Layout**

Title top-left at left margin. Below, two panels: a left waterfall chart panel (about 53% wide) under a dark blue header bar, and a right panel (about 37% wide) with a header bar, a light grey bullet list and a dark navy callout box at the bottom. A thin grey rule, source/notes text, small logos at bottom-left and page number at bottom-right form the footer.

**Visuals**

Waterfall chart of 2027E net income in $ millions with six columns on a thin light-grey baseline, no axes or gridlines. Columns: WaFd ’27E consensus net income $250 (cyan bar); EverBank ’27E net income $438 (royal blue floating bar stacked above $250); Fully phased in synergies $100 (light green floating bar); NII & merger adjustments $50 (light green floating bar); CDI amortization ($49) (light pink floating bar); Pro forma net income $788 (cyan bar). Value labels in bold navy sit above each bar. Above the chart, a row labelled ’27E EPS has a dark navy oval pill $3.37 at left, a large royal-blue right-pointing arrow labelled ~29% in white, and a navy oval pill $4.34 at right. Below the chart, a row labelled ’27E ROATCE has navy oval pills 10.5% (under WaFd), 11.9% (under EverBank) and 15%+ (under pro forma). Beneath the pills: a green WaFd starburst logo under the 10.5% pill, a blue EverBank logo under the 11.9% pill, and both logos separated by a vertical bar under the 15%+ pill. Right panel has seven-plus bullets with royal-blue round bullet dots. Small WaFd and EverBank logos with a divider appear in the footer bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Calibri-style sans-serif in bright blue #1751E8, about 4.8% of slide height. Panel header bars in royal navy #113DAE with white bold Arial text about 2.2%. Bullet panel in light grey #F2F2F2 with navy #142571 Arial text about 1.85%, bold lead phrases then regular text after em dashes. Callout box in dark navy #142571 with white bold italic Arial 2.4%, rounded corners about 1.5% radius. Chart bars flat colours with no shadows. Footer text tiny blue #1751E8 Arial; page number in cyan #00A3E0.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Presents capital ratios as a simple ordered bar series with a clear threshold callout, making the well-capitalized claim immediately checkable.
- Review: Slides 6, 11 and 25 show disciplined execution, with large key numbers, readable bars and donuts, and clear takeaways.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent header-bar system, the large-number KPI tiles on slides 6 and 25, the blue-green palette, and the clear section dividers and sequence from overview to rationale to appendix.

**Text**

✓ Multiple sources of readily available liquidity

**Layout**

Title top-left, left-aligned. Three columns below: two bullet-list panels with blue header bars and a bar chart column on the right. Below them, a section heading with a rule and a row of five equal target cards. Footer has a grey rule, logos at bottom left, source/note text, and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart (no axis labels, thin grey vertical axis line) with five dark navy bars: TCE / TA 7.0%, CET1 9.8%, Tier 1 Capital 11.2%, Total Capital 12.6%, Tier 1 Leverage 8.1%. Value labels sit to the right of the bars. A callout box with a navy outline and pale fill, reading '>10% CET1 1–2 quarters post-close', sits beside the CET1 bar. Five pale-blue cards with drop shadows hold the targets. Navy check marks mark each bullet. Footer shows two small logos: a blue stylised 'E' mark and a green circular emblem, separated by a thin vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, Calibri bold, about 4.8% of slide height. Header bars are royal blue #113DAE with white Arial bold text, about 2.2%. Panels have a pale blue tint #ECF1FC. Body text is Arial about 1.84%, in dark #162727 with navy #142571 bold key phrases. Bars are navy #142571. Target cards are #D1DCFA with soft shadows; their big numbers are Arial bold navy about 2.8% and their labels bold italic about 1.7%. Header bars have slightly rounded corners. Footer text is small blue #1751E8 Arial. Page number is cyan #00A3E0.

**Layout**

Title top-left. Upper left: a credit performance panel with a dark blue label block, two logo tiles and two checkmark bullet pairs. Upper right: a blue header bar over three equal grey tiles. Lower left: a light grey chart panel with three bars. Lower right: a blue header bar over a two-bar CRE concentration chart. Footer rule, logos bottom left, source notes, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: dark blue block (#113DAE) with white label; light blue tile (#A2B9F6) with blue EverBank chevron logo; mint green tile (#B7EAC8) with green WaFd starburst logo; thin grey horizontal rules separate and bound the bullet rows. Three grey tiles hold the EverBank logo (blue chevron with wordmark), the green WaFd logo, and the KRX text. Bar chart in a light grey panel: three vertical bars on a thin baseline, ACL 1.09% bright cyan-blue, Fair value mark 0.77% pale mint, Loss absorption capacity 1.86% navy; y-axis unit label '% of total loans'. Right chart: green bar 342% with WaFd logo below, navy bar 266% with WaFd | EverBank logos below, and a navy bracket line from above the first bar to an arrow pointing down at the second bar. Footer: EverBank chevron logo | WaFd logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #1751E8 Calibri-style sans-serif. Header bars are royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text and small rounded corners. Body text navy #142571 Arial regular. Key figures bold blue #1851E9. Check marks blue and green. KRX and 13bps in dark #162727. Grey tiles #F2F2F2. Footer text small blue, page number cyan #00A3E0. **Layout**

Title top-left over two side-by-side blue-outlined panels (left: focus-area grid; right: diligence process and stats), with a full-width light grey band of investor logos below. Footer has a thin grey rule, small corner logos at left, notes text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: grey header band with EverBank logo (blue stacked-bar mark with dark grey wordmark), a blue plus sign and WaFd logo (green line-art emblem with green wordmark). A 4-row by 3-column grid of light grey cards with drop shadows, each with a blue circled checkmark at its upper left. Right panel: grey band with underlined heading and five partner logos (SRA in blue with diamond-pattern mark, grey CLAYTON, Cornerstone Advisors with teal/green cube mark, black curinos, green CBRE). Below, two columns: a pale blue column for EverBank and a pale green column for WaFd, each with three white stat boxes with thin outlines of the column colour. Bottom grey band with six investor logos: Stone Point Capital (small caps serif, orange underline), Warburg Pincus (serif small caps), RCP Reverence Capital Partners (navy square with white RCP), Sixth Street (blue/green 6 mark with dark blue text), Bayview Asset Management (building mark, blue and green text), TIAA (navy square mark with text). Footer has tiny EverBank and WaFd icons separated by a thin vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings in bright blue #1751E8, bold Calibri for slide title and bold Arial for headings, with the title about 4.8% of slide height. Card labels are bold Arial blue at about 2% height. Green accents #47B26D and #37AC61 for WaFd. Cards are #F2F2F2 with soft drop shadows; the panels have a 2px blue outline. Investor tagline is bold italic navy #142571. Notes are tiny blue Arial.

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left. Below, two panels: a wide left scatter chart panel (about 53% width) with blue header bar, and a right panel (about 36% width) with blue header bar and a five-row two-column table. Thin grey footer rule with combined logo at bottom-left, three source/note lines indented at left-centre, and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Scatter plot with y-axis P/TBV from 0.50x to 3.00x (ticks every 0.50x) and x-axis 2027E ROATCE from 6.0% to 21.0% (ticks every 3.0%), dark grey axis lines at left and bottom. About 50 light grey circular dots (peer banks) scattered, roughly rising from lower-left (about 8%, 0.8x) to upper-right (about 21%, 2.95x). A thin navy regression line runs from lower left (about 6%, 0.6x) to upper right (about 21.5%, 2.6x). An equation box with thin navy outline at top-left of the plot contains 'y = 12.68x – 0.16 / R2 = 62%'. A green outlined starburst/badge logo (WaFd) sits at about 10% ROATCE, 1.15x, next to a light green dot on the regression line at about 10.5%, 1.2x. A green arrow goes up vertically from that dot to about 1.8x then right horizontally to a dark navy dot on the regression line at about 15.5%, 1.8x. A blue dashed arrow runs from the combined-company logo (blue stylized E mark, a vertical divider, and green badge, with superscript (2)) at about 11-13%, 2.0x to the navy dot. Right table has grey label cells and pale blue value cells; a thin black line separates row 3 and row 4 in the value column. The final value ~44% is circled by a red ellipse outline. Footer logos: blue E mark, thin divider, green badge.

Highlights

Brand:

Core Lending Verticals
Relationship driven, commercial lending across a diversified set of national industry verticals
Commitment to areas with potential to build scale and win without overextending across smaller business lines

Focus on loans with attractive risk adjusted returns within industry verticals that have demonstrated low credit losses

Energy Lending

**Layout**

White slide with a title across the top beside a circled number. Two columns below: a narrower left Highlights column (about 5% to 45% wide) with a bullet list and two stat boxes at the bottom, and a wider right Overview column (47% to 95%) with three stat boxes and a Core Lending Verticals list of six heading and description pairs. A thin grey rule, a footer with logos at left, source and notes in the centre-left, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A blue ring with the number 2 sits left of the title. Three pale lavender stat cards with drop shadows sit in a row under the Overview header. The first holds the EverBank logo (blue stacked-bars icon and 'EverBank' wordmark in dark grey) under the label 'Brand:'. The second shows $39.4B / Loans and the third $9.6B / Deposits. Two similar shadowed cards at the bottom of the left panel show 5.82% / Average Current Yield and 0.06% / Average Annual NCO Rate(1). The left panel has a light grey translucent background. Blue round bullets mark the main items and a grey round bullet marks the sub-bullet. Six pale blue-grey strips sit behind the vertical descriptions. A thin grey horizontal line sits above the footer. The footer has a small blue EverBank icon, a thin vertical divider and a small green circular emblem at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Calibri-style sans-serif, royal blue #1751E8, about 4.8% of slide height. Section header bars are solid dark royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial centred text, about 2.2% of slide height, and slightly rounded corners. Body text is Arial regular in navy #142571, about 2.2% of slide height, with generous line spacing. Stat numbers are bold Arial in navy #142571, about 4.4% of slide height. Stat cards are filled #E2E7FA with soft drop shadows. Vertical headings are bold Arial in blue #1650E8 at about 2% of slide height. Descriptions are Arial regular in near-black #181818 at about 1.85% of slide height on #ECF1FC strips. Footer text is small Arial in blue #1751E8. Page number is Calibri in cyan #00A3E0.

36

**Layout**

Title top-left over a full-width content area split into two stacked sections, each with a dark blue header bar spanning about 90% of the width. Each section has a legend row, three donut charts (WaFd, EverBank, combined) joined by large + and = signs, logos under each donut, and a three-column table of metrics with row labels on the left. Footnotes sit at the bottom left, a small logo pair at the bottom-left corner, and the slide number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six donut charts. Top row, loan portfolio composition, segments clockwise from top: CRE (pale lavender), Multifamily (bright blue), 1–4 family (navy), C&I (periwinkle), C&D (cyan), Consumer/other (grey). $20B WaFd: CRE 17%, Multifamily 22%, 1–4 family 34%, C&I 14%, C&D 11%, Consumer/other 2%. $37B EverBank: CRE 9%, Multifamily 13%, 1–4 family 20%, C&I 58%. $58B combined: CRE 12%, Multifamily 16%, 1–4 family 25%, C&I 42%, C&D 4%. Bottom row, deposit breakdown, segments: Financial centers (pale lavender), Consumer direct (bright blue), Commercial direct & specialty (navy), Sweeps (periwinkle), Other (grey). $21B WaFd: Financial centers 70%, Consumer direct 0% (not labelled), Commercial 22%, Other 7%. $38B EverBank: Financial centers 27%, Consumer direct 47%, Commercial 13%, Sweeps 10%, Other 3%. $59B combined: Financial centers 43%, Consumer direct 30%, Commercial 16%, Sweeps 7%, Other 4%. Each donut has a bold blue dollar total in its centre. Large blue + signs between the first and second donuts and blue = signs between the second and third, in both rows. Logos: green WaFd star-burst logo with 'WaFd' text, EverBank 'E' logo with 'EverBank' text, and a combined pair of icons separated by a thin vertical line with a (2) superscript. Metric cells are rounded-looking shaded boxes with a drop shadow in tints from lavender to light cyan to near-white. Small square legend swatches precede legend labels. A thin grey horizontal rule sits above the footer. Footer corner has the EverBank E icon, a thin divider and the WaFd icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel behind the loan section. White background; the top section body sits on a light grey #F2F2F2 panel. Title in bright blue #1751E8, bold Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Section header bars in dark blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text about 2.2% of height, with slightly rounded top corners. Donut labels are white bold Arial at 1.7%. Center totals are bold Arial in #1751E8 at 3.3%. Table values and row labels are bold italic Arial in navy #142571 at 1.7%. Table cell fills are #BFC9F4, light lavender and light cyan with soft shadows. Legend text is Arial 1.7% in the matching segment colours. Plus and equals signs are Franklin Gothic Book at 8.3% in #1751E8. Footnotes are Arial 1.1% in #1751E8. Slide number is Calibri in #00A3E0.

### Slide 37

**Text**

Investor Area of Focus: Non-Depository Financial Institution Loans
The NDFI portfolio reflects robust / diversification
Exposure spans multiple asset / classes within verticals, mitigating / concentration risk and enhancing / resilience through cycles
Asset-backed and secured by / investor commitments or portfolio / assets, reducing correlation to / economic cycles
No historical loan loss within the / NDFI portfolio
STRUCTURED MORTGAGE (23%)
Acquired business in 2012 from MetLife
Traditional mortgage warehouse and MSR / financing facilities (75% and 25%, respectively)
Substantial majority are uncommitted facilities
No historical losses or criticized loans
LENDER FINANCE (10%)

**Layout**

Title top-left. A row of four equal rounded callout boxes sits under the title. Below, a central donut chart is flanked by two text blocks on the left (Structured Mortgage above, Corp Debt Finance below) and two on the right (Lender Finance above, Fund Finance below). Each block has a coloured bar and heading, with dashed connectors to the donut. A thin grey rule runs above the footer. Footer has logos bottom-left, source notes at left-centre and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Central donut ring with four segments and a white centre. Clockwise from the top: Structured Mortgage (teal-blue #0073AC, left upper), Lender Finance (light green #4CCB78, small top segment), Fund Finance (bright blue #1751E8, right side and bottom right), Corporate Debt Finance (navy #142571, bottom left). The ring has a soft drop shadow. Segment names are curved along the ring: the Lender Finance label is green, the others are in segment colours on the ring. Four round dots mark connector points on the ring: one on Structured Mortgage, one on Lender Finance, one on Fund Finance and one on Corporate Debt Finance. Dashed thin connector lines in each segment's colour run from the dots to the heading bars. Thick horizontal bars sit above each heading: teal-blue top-left, navy bottom-left, green top-right, blue bottom-right. Four pale lavender rounded rectangles with a light periwinkle outline hold the callouts. Bullets are dark teal-grey filled circles. Footer has an EverBank 'E' logo, a thin vertical divider and a small green circular emblem logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, bold Calibri-style sans-serif, about 4.8% of slide height. Callout boxes #EEF1FC with a light blue border, large corner radius, text in Arial with navy #142571 bold highlights and #162727 regular. Headings are bold Arial caps about 2.6% in segment colours. Body is Arial about 1.84% in #2B4F4F with bold key phrases in segment colours (#0073AC, #2FA254, #142571, #1751E8). Centre figure is bold Arial about 6.7% in #162727. Footer source text is tiny Arial in blue #1751E8, and the page number is in cyan #00A3E0.

**Layout**

Title top-left, left-aligned. Left column (about 5%-38% wide) holds a blue header bar 'CRE Overview' over a pale blue panel with five bullets. Right area (43%-95%) has a blue header bar, a chart title and legend, three donut charts side by side with logos beneath, then a 3-column by 4-row grid of metric boxes with row labels at left. Thin grey rule above the footer; small logos at bottom-left, source notes at bottom centre-left, page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three donut charts of CRE distribution by property type (%). Segments run clockwise from the top: Multifamily (pale periwinkle #BFC9F4), Warehouse/Industrial (bright blue #1751E8), Office (dark navy #142571), Retail (mid periwinkle #7497F1), Other (cyan #00AAFF). Chart 1 (WaFd, centre $8B (1)): Multifamily 54%, Warehouse/Industrial 13%, Office 9%, Retail 4%, Other 19%. Chart 2 (EverBank, centre $8B (1)): Multifamily 63%, Warehouse/Industrial 15%, Office 9%, Retail 9%, Other 4%. Chart 3 (combined, centre $17B (1)): Multifamily 58%, Warehouse/Industrial 14%, Office 9%, Retail 7%, Other 12%. Legend above the charts uses square swatches in the series colours. Logos: green WaFd starburst wordmark under chart 1; blue EverBank 'E' mark with dark 'EverBank' wordmark under chart 2; green WaFd emblem, a vertical divider and blue EverBank 'E' mark under chart 3 with a (2) superscript. The 4x3 grid of pale rectangular metric boxes (#F3F6FD) has soft drop shadows. Small EverBank 'E' and WaFd emblem logos with a divider sit at bottom-left. A thin grey horizontal rule runs above the footer. Bullets are blue round dots.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #1751E8, Calibri Bold, about 4.8% of slide height. Section header bars are solid royal blue #113DAE with white bold Arial text and slightly rounded corners. Left panel fill is pale blue #ECF1FC. Body text is dark navy #142571 Arial regular, about 2% of height. Chart title and the big donut-centre figures are bold blue #1751E8. Metric labels and values are bold italic navy Arial. Footnotes are small blue Arial. The page number is cyan #00A3E0 Calibri. Boxes have subtle drop shadows; no gradients.

**Layout**

Title top-left, with a '$ in millions' subtitle and a legend beneath it. Below is a 3-column by 2-row grid of small bar-chart panels, each with a pale header bar, a grey-dot ratio label, a row of five ratio pills, a bar chart and year labels. A thin grey rule near the bottom separates the footer: logos at left, source and notes text in the middle, page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six bar charts, each with five vertical bars for 2023-2027 on a light grey baseline, with bold dollar value labels above each bar and no y-axis. 2023-2025 bars are dark navy (Actual) and 2026-2027 bars are bright blue (Management Forecast). Above each chart is a row of five rounded oval pills with white bold percentages: navy for 2023-2025 and bright blue for 2026-2027. Legend: navy square for Actual, bright blue square for Management Forecast. Each ratio label has a small light grey oval dot to its left. The panel header bars are pale blue-white rounded rectangles with a thin light blue outline. Chart values: Net Interest Income $730, $896, $1,121, $1,229, $1,335 with NIM 2.05%, 2.34%, 2.62%, 2.65%, 2.74%. Provisions $15, $25, $62, $25, $54 with 0.05%, 0.09%, 0.20%, 0.07%, 0.14%. Noninterest Income $166, $87, $109, $99, $115 with 19%, 9%, 9%, 7%, 8%. Noninterest Expense $760, $643, $746, $706, $737 with 2.09%, 1.66%, 1.73%, 1.51%, 1.49%. PPNR $136, $341, $484, $622, $713 with 0.49%, 0.88%, 1.12%, 1.32%, 1.45%. Net Income to Common $89, $186, $262, $394, $438 with 0.28%, 0.59%, 0.71%, 0.94%, 0.98%. Footer: the blue EverBank 'E' logo, a thin vertical divider and a green circular emblem logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #1751E8, bold Calibri, about 4.8% of slide height. Panel headers in bold Arial, bright blue #1751E8, about 1.8%. Body, labels and values in Arial, black, about 1.7%; values are bold. Pills use bold white Arial at 1.6%. Footnotes in small Arial at 1.1%, blue #1751E8. Page number in Calibri, cyan #00A3E0. Panel header fill is #F4F7FE with a thin light blue outline and slightly rounded corners. Bars are flat with no shadows or gradients. Navy is #142571 and bright blue is #1751E8.

Legend markers: navy and blue squares for Actual and Management Forecast; dark grey square for Cash, light grey square for Securities; light grey ovals beside each badge-metric label. Footer logos: a blue EverBank 'E' mark and a green circular emblem separated by a thin vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a bright blue title and a restrained navy and blue palette. Panel headers are pale blue (#F4F7FE) rounded rectangles with a thin light-blue outline and bold blue centred text. Sans-serif Arial for body text and Calibri bold for the title. Chart labels are bold black; the badges are pill-shaped with no shadows.

Bottom left holds two small logos: a blue stylized 'E' mark, a thin vertical divider and a green outlined star/seal emblem.

### Slide 43

**Text**

+
STRONGER TOGETHER
43

**Layout**

Centered vertical stack in the middle of the slide: EverBank logo at top, a plus sign, the WaFd logo, then the tagline 'STRONGER TOGETHER' centered below. Small slide number in the bottom right corner. No title at top; wide margins on both sides.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract background image of flowing, translucent swooshing ribbons with fine parallel lines: blue ribbons sweep from the top-left corner toward the centre, and green ribbons sweep from the centre toward the bottom-right corner, fading into near-white. The EverBank logo (a blue stacked chevron/E-shaped mark in royal blue #2B5CE6 followed by the wordmark 'EverBank' in dark charcoal thin sans-serif) sits at the upper centre. The WaFd logo (green outlined interlocking starburst/knot mark followed by bold green 'WaFd' wordmark) sits below the plus sign.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed image of blue and green flowing translucent ribbons on a near-white #FAFAFA field. White to very light grey background #FAFAFA with soft blue (#1F6FD6 to #A9C6EE) and green (#2E9A3E to #CDE8D0) gradient ribbons with thin white highlight lines. Tagline and plus sign in dark navy #142571, Calibri Bold in capitals, large at about 7.4% of slide height. Page number small in cyan-blue #00A3E0, Calibri. No shadows or borders.
